## MAIS Manim V2 EvidenceHarness Scene.update_frame Payload Slice - 00:07 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 by aligning standalone EvidenceHarness diagnostics with the pure Manim `Scene.update_frame` source contract.
- Source grounding: the Manim source architecture distillation identifies `Scene.update_frame` as the per-frame director loop that increments scene time, updates mobjects, checks skip/force-draw/window paths, captures render groups, and optionally sleeps to sync preview timing. The browser port needs this frame-loop evidence visible through stable QA attributes.
- TDD RED: extended `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathEvidenceHarness.test.ts` with a browser-QA contract asserting `manimUpdateFrame*` snapshot fields and `data-viz-manim-update-frame-*` attributes. - Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathEvidenceHarness.ts` now accepts optional `updateFrameEvidence`, builds `buildSceneUpdateFramePlan(...)` from runtime timeline/update-policy/render-group state, and flattens action, capture/dispatch flags, dt, force-draw, render group count/ids, next scene time, skip, sleep, and summary into the evidence snapshot.
- Changed: EvidenceHarness now reuses `sceneUpdateFrameDataAttributes()` so standalone evidence attributes match the existing pure `Scene.update_frame` DOM contract.

- Mobile browser verification blocked before test execution: `npx playwright test tests/e2e/visualization-overlap.spec.ts --project=mobile-chrome` failed on the same Next webServer compile blocker before reaching browser assertions.

## MAIS Manim V2 EvidenceHarness Mobject State-Payload Slice - 22:09 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.

## MAIS Manim V2 Scene Pointer Controls Slice - 20:11 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by extracting a pure pointer-control contract for Manim `Scene.on_mouse_motion()`, `Scene.on_mouse_drag()`, `Scene.on_mouse_press()`, `Scene.on_mouse_release()`, and `Scene.on_mouse_scroll()`.
- Source grounding: local `/Users/dongpinhu/Downloads/manim-master.zip` `manim-master/manimlib/scene/scene.py` shows motion asserts a window, moves `mouse_point`, dispatches `MouseMotionEvent`, then pans 3D with `increment_theta` / `increment_phi` or shifts the frame when pan is pressed. Drag moves `mouse_drag_point` and applies `drag_to_pan` before dispatching `MouseDragEvent`. Press moves `mouse_drag_point` and dispatches only. Release dispatches only. Scroll dispatches `MouseScrollEvent`, then scales the frame about the pointer unless propagation is stopped.

- Type-check correction: the first `npm run type-check` after implementation caught one S06 test-local typing mismatch because `mathScenePointerControls.test.ts` omitted the implementation's `modifiers` and `offset` fields from its local plan type. ## MAIS Manim V2 wait Frame update_frame Bridge Slice - 19:49 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by tightening `Scene.wait()` / `Scene.wait_until()` evidence so every wait tick carries the same `Scene.update_frame(dt)` contract as animation playback, while presenter-mode `hold_loop()` records update-frame-only frames without `emit_frame()`.
- Source grounding: local `/Users/dongpinhu/Downloads/manim-master.zip` `manim-master/manimlib/scene/scene.py` shows `wait()` calls `pre_play()`, `update_mobjects(dt=0)`, then either enters `hold_loop()` for presenter mode or iterates `get_wait_time_progression()`. In the timeline branch each wait sample computes `dt`, calls `update_frame(dt)`, calls `emit_frame()`, and breaks after a satisfied `stop_condition`. `hold_loop()` repeatedly calls `update_frame(dt=1 / camera.fps)` while holding, without calling `emit_frame()`.

Ordinary waits and `wait_until` frames set `callsEmitFrame=true`; skipped wait frames still call `emit_frame` in the contract but do not write a frame because `update_frame` resolves to `skip-return`; presenter `hold_loop` frames set `callsEmitFrame=false` and `writesFrame=false` while preserving `updateFrame.action="capture"` evidence.

## MAIS Manim V2 update_frame Frame Director Slice - 19:44 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by adding a pure contract for Manim `Scene.update_frame(dt, force_draw=False)` frame-director behavior, then bridging it into `progress_through_animations()` frame evidence.
- Source grounding: local `/Users/dongpinhu/Downloads/manim-master.zip` `manim-master/manimlib/scene/scene.py` shows `update_frame()` first calls `increment_time(dt)`, then `update_mobjects(dt)`, then returns early when `skip_animations` is true and `force_draw` is false. It raises `EndScene` when the window is closing, dispatches events and returns when a window has no undrawn event on a zero-dt non-force frame, otherwise calls `camera.capture(*render_groups)`. With a window and non-skipped animations, it sleeps for `max((time - virtual_animation_start_time) - (time.time() - real_animation_start_time), 0)` to synchronize preview timing.

- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneProgressThroughAnimations.ts` now builds a `MathSceneUpdateFramePlan` for every sampled animation frame, so `progress_through_animations()` evidence records the exact Manim `update_frame` branch alongside animation `update_mobjects()` and `interpolate(alpha)` calls.

## MAIS Manim V2 Scene.save_state History Cap Slice - 18:34 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by tightening the pure authoring history model around Manim `Scene.save_state()`, `undo()`, and `redo()` behavior.
- Source grounding: local `/Users/dongpinhu/Downloads/manim-master.zip` `manim-master/manimlib/scene/scene.py` sets `max_num_saved_states = 50`, initializes `undo_stack` and `redo_stack`, and implements `save_state()` by creating a `SceneState`, returning early when the new state `mobjects_match` the latest saved state, clearing `redo_stack`, appending to `undo_stack`, then popping the oldest entry when the stack exceeds `max_num_saved_states`.
- TDD RED: extended `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneHistory.test.ts` with two failing cases: unchanged adjacent authoring state should no-op instead of adding an undo entry, and a small `maxUndoEntries` cap should drop the oldest undo state. ## MAIS Manim V2 always-method Children-First Runtime Slice - 15:45 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by making the actual `always-method` runtime pass follow Manim's children-first `Mobject.update(dt)` family traversal semantics, not only the QA execution-plan evidence.
- Source grounding: Manim updates children before the parent mobject's own updater callbacks, so a parent-level relationship updater must see a child's current-frame relationship result when both are active in the same family.
- TDD RED: extended `mathAlwaysMethodUpdater.test.ts` with a parent curve `path`, child moving point `probe`, and external vector `anchor`. The expected behavior is `probe.nextTo(anchor, UP)` first, then `path.nextTo(probe, UP)`. The focused run failed as intended because `path` saw the stale `probe` position and stayed near the origin.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathUpdaterRegistry.ts` now builds a postorder object traversal from `buildMobjectFamilyIndex()` and applies active `always-method` updaters against a mutable current-frame object graph.
- Changed: the traversal includes a sorted fallback across all object ids after top-level roots so malformed/cyclic family metadata stays inspectable without dropping updater execution.

## MAIS Manim V2 Scene Membership Lifecycle Slice - 15:04 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by making `FadeIn`, `GrowFromCenter`, and `FadeOut` affect scene membership, not only opacity/progress metadata.

MAIS now applies that lifecycle to R3F render groups through the pure scene graph layer.
- Scope confirmation: edits stayed inside S06-owned `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneGraph.ts`, `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneGraph.test.ts`, `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneRuntimeState.ts`, `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneRuntimeState.test.ts`, and this S06 log. The focused run failed as intended because the runtime still rendered `late-vector` and `grow-label` before their introduction.

- Changed: `mathSceneRuntimeState.ts` now computes membership from `runtimeScene.timeline` and `runtimeTimeline.elapsedSeconds`, stores it on `state.sceneGraph.membership`, and uses the excluded object ids for runtime render groups and scene graph summary.

## MAIS Manim V2 Runtime Lag-Then-Rate Transform Slice - 14:02 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by correcting the runtime `transformObject` family interpolation order to match Manim `interpolate_mobject()` semantics.
- Source grounding: Manim computes the animation's raw alpha, then each family member's lagged sub-alpha, then applies `rate_func` before interpolation. MAIS runtime previously applied the transform rate function at the whole-plan level before family `lagRatio`, which made lagged family members ease too early.

- Changed: `mathAnimationRuntime.ts` now tracks plan progress as `{ rawProgress, progress, rateFunction }`, preserves eased plan-level `progress` for frame summaries, and computes node interpolation progress as `applyRateFunction(rateFunction, laggedProgress(rawProgress, ...))`.

## MAIS Manim V2 Transform path_arc Animate Runtime Slice - 14:29 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by connecting Manim-style `Transform(path_arc=...)` semantics to the MAIS `.animate` target-state builder, durable scene animation plans, and runtime interpolation.
- Source grounding: Manim `Transform` accepts `path_func` / `path_arc` so point data can move along authored geometric paths instead of always lerping through a straight chord. MAIS already had pure `mathPathFunctions.ts` support for `straightPath`, `pathAlongArc`, and `resolveTransformPathFunction`; the missing piece was serializing and consuming that path spec from `.animate` and scene-authored plans.

- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathAnimationBuilder.ts` now accepts optional `path` in `MathAnimateBuildOptions` and clones it into the generated transform step, preserving existing straight-path behavior when omitted.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneAnimationPlans.ts` now passes scene-authored `planSpec.path` through to the ordinary `MathAnimateBuilder.build()` path.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathAnimationRuntime.ts` now resolves `plan.step.path` through `resolveTransformPathFunction()` and applies the selected path function to point, polyline, vector, and surface point interpolation while leaving style/uniform interpolation unchanged.

## MAIS Manim V2 FadeIn/FadeOut/GrowFromCenter Timeline Slice - 14:55 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by promoting Manim creation/removal primitives (`FadeIn`, `FadeOut`, `GrowFromCenter`) from manually sampled frame kinds into durable `AnimationStep` timeline semantics.
- Source grounding: Manim creation/removal animations are ordinary timeline-owned animations. They have easing/rate functions, own a Mobject family during playback, suspend ordinary updaters, expose progress for scrubbing/tests, and are later used by renderer adapters to drive opacity/scale/draw frames.
- TDD RED 1: added `mathCreationPrimitives.test.ts` coverage expecting scene-authored `{ type: "fadeInObject" }`, `{ type: "fadeOutObject" }`, and `{ type: "growFromCenter" }` steps to produce `fadeIn`, `fadeOut`, and `growFromCenter` creation primitive plan rows. The focused run failed as intended because the plan emitted `[]`.
- TDD RED 2: added `mathTimeline.test.ts` coverage expecting creation/removal beats to expose active step, active concept, eased local progress, and object-level progress. The focused run failed as intended because `timelineObjectProgress()` returned `1` instead of local `0.5`.
- TDD RED 3: added `mathUpdaterSuspension.test.ts` coverage expecting a `fadeOutObject` beat to suspend ordinary updaters on the owned Mobject family. The focused run failed as intended with phase `open` instead of `animation`.

- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathCreationPrimitives.ts` now maps those authored timeline beats into `fadeIn`, `fadeOut`, and `growFromCenter` primitive plan rows.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathTimeline.ts` now treats creation/removal beats as object-owning steps and fixes repeated-object progress so a later `FadeOut(label)` can report its own local alpha after an earlier `FadeIn(label)`.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathRateFunctions.ts` now honors authored easing for the new creation/removal beats.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathUpdaterSuspension.ts` now treats the new beats as animation-owned so ordinary updaters on the target family are suspended during playback.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathValueTracker.ts` now creates object progress trackers for authored fade/grow beats.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathEvidenceHarness.ts` now resolves active concept evidence from fade/grow target objects so semantic formula/object highlighting can follow these beats.

## MAIS Manim V2 ODE Trajectory Evidence Slice - 01:47 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by adding a deterministic ODE trajectory contract inspired by Manim dynamic-system `ode_solution_points`: solve/cache math-time trajectories first, then let runtime animation consume semantic samples.
- Source grounding: Manim dynamic-system scenes separate vector fields, ODE solution points, current-state markers, traces, and camera movement. This slice ports the source-level trajectory/cache idea into pure TypeScript for browser QA; it does not add Python Manim, SciPy, or new runtime dependencies.
- TDD RED: added `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathOdeTrajectory.test.ts` and a Canvas source-contract test; first focused run failed because `mathOdeTrajectory.ts` and Canvas `data-viz-manim-ode-*` evidence did not exist.

- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathEvidenceHarness.ts` now summarizes scene ODE trajectories and emits `data-viz-manim-ode-trajectory-count`, sample, finite-sample, stopped, tail-sample, and summary evidence.

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.

- Changed: the clock now publishes `setSteppedManimFrameStep(nextFrame)`, updates `previousRuntimeStateRef.current = nextFrame.runtimeState`, and advances elapsed seconds/frame index from that same frame.
- Changed: Canvas now renders/evidences the stepped frame when it matches the current scene, elapsed time, and frame index, while preserving the zero-delta fallback for paused, scrubbed, reset, and scene-change states.
- Changed: reset/scene-change paths clear the stepped frame so stale live playback state does not leak across selected scene specs.

## MAIS Manim V2 Mobject.rotate Animate Operation Slice - 11:52 HKT

- Session: S06 Visualization lead.
- Objective: continue MAIS Manim 第二版 source-level Manim distillation by adding a Manim-style `Mobject.rotate` operation to the existing `.animate` target-state builder and scene-authored animation plan path.

The focused run failed as intended because the target vector stayed at its original coordinates.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneTypes.ts` now includes `rotate` in `MathSceneAnimateOperationSpec`.
- Changed: `components/visualizations/three/manim/mathSceneAnimationPlans.ts` now dispatches scene-authored `rotate` operations into the ordinary `MathAnimateBuilder.rotate()` path, so authored scene specs and direct builder use share the same target-state behavior.
